regard t/uni006F the characteristics /uni006Ff the specific asset /uni006Fr liability. The Gr/uni006Fup measures and rec/uni006Fgnises the f/uni006Fll/uni006Fwing assets and liabilities at fair value /uni006Fn a recurring basis after initial rec/uni006Fgniti/uni006Fn:

- ∞ Financial assets at fair value thr/uni006Fugh pr/uni006Ffit &amp; l/uni006Fss (investment); and
- ∞ Derivative financial instruments at fair value asset /uni006Fr (liability). Hedging ineffectiveness being rec/uni006Fgnised thr/uni006Fugh pr/uni006Ffit &amp; l/uni006Fss.

## a. Fair value hierarchy

AASB 13: Fair Value Measurement requires the discl/uni006Fsure /uni006Ff fair value inf/uni006Frmati/uni006Fn by level /uni006Ff the fair value hierarchy, which categ/uni006Frises fair value measurements int/uni006F /uni006Fne /uni006Ff three p/uni006Fssible levels based /uni006Fn the l/uni006Fwest level that an input that is significant t/uni006F the measurement can be categ/uni006Frised int/uni006F as f/uni006Fll/uni006Fws:

| Level 1  Level 2  Level 3  Measurements based /uni006Fn qu/uni006Fted  prices (unadjusted) in active markets  f/uni006Fr identical assets /uni006Fr liabilities that  the entity can access at the  measurement date  Measurements based /uni006Fn inputs /uni006Fther  than qu/uni006Fted prices included in Level 1  that are /uni006Fbservable f/uni006Fr the asset /uni006Fr  liability, either directly /uni006Fr indirectly.  Measurements based /uni006Fn  un/uni006Fbservable inputs f/uni006Fr the asset /uni006Fr  liability   |
|------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------|

The fair values /uni006Ff assets and liabilities that are n/uni006Ft traded in an active market are determined using /uni006Fne /uni006Fr m/uni006Fre valuati/uni006Fn techniques. These valuati/uni006Fn techniques maximise, t/uni006F the extent p/uni006Fssible, the use /uni006Ff /uni006Fbservable market data. If all significant inputs required t/uni006F measure fair value are /uni006Fbservable, the asset /uni006Fr liability is included in Level 2. If /uni006Fne /uni006Fr m/uni006Fre significant inputs are n/uni006Ft based /uni006Fn /uni006Fbservable market data, the asset /uni006Fr liability is included in Level 3.

## b. Valuati/uni006Fn techniques

The Gr/uni006Fup selects a valuati/uni006Fn technique that is appr/uni006Fpriate in the circumstances and f/uni006Fr which sufficient data is available t/uni006F measure fair value. The availability /uni006Ff sufficient and relevant data

primarily depends /uni006Fn the specific characteristics /uni006Ff the asset /uni006Fr liability being measured. The valuati/uni006Fn techniques selected by the Gr/uni006Fup are c/uni006Fnsistent with /uni006Fne /uni006Fr m/uni006Fre /uni006Ff the f/uni006Fll/uni006Fwing valuati/uni006Fn appr/uni006Faches:

- ∞ Inc/uni006Fme appr/uni006Fach: valuati/uni006Fn techniques that c/uni006Fnvert estimated future cash fl/uni006Fws /uni006Fr inc/uni006Fme and expenses int/uni006F a single disc/uni006Funted present value.
- ∞ C/uni006Fst appr/uni006Fach: valuati/uni006Fn techniques that reflect the current replacement c/uni006Fst /uni006Ff an asset at its current service capacity.

Each valuati/uni006Fn technique requires inputs that reflect the assumpti/uni006Fns that buyers and sellers w/uni006Fuld use when pricing the asset /uni006Fr liability, including assumpti/uni006Fns ab/uni006Fut risks. When selecting a valuati/uni006Fn technique, the Gr/uni006Fup gives pri/uni006Frity t/uni006F th/uni006Fse techniques that maximise the use /uni006Ff /uni006Fbservable inputs and minimise the use /uni006Ff un/uni006Fbservable inputs. ## 1.9 Hedge acc/uni006Funting

## 6.1 Impairment /uni006Ff financial assets

The Gr/uni006Fup rec/uni006Fgnises a l/uni006Fss all/uni006Fwance f/uni006Fr ECL /uni006Fn financial assets which are either measured at am/uni006Frtised c/uni006Fst /uni006Fr fair value thr/uni006Fugh /uni006Fther c/uni006Fmprehensive inc/uni006Fme. The Gr/uni006Fup has ad/uni006Fpted a three-stage m/uni006Fdel f/uni006Fr ECL pr/uni006Fvisi/uni006Fning:

## Stage 1: 12 m/uni006Fnths ECL

Where there has n/uni006Ft been a significant increase in exp/uni006Fsure t/uni006F credit risk since initial rec/uni006Fgniti/uni006Fn, a 12-m/uni006Fnth ECL all/uni006Fwance is estimated. This represents a p/uni006Frti/uni006Fn /uni006Ff the l/uni006Fan receivable lifetime ECL that is attributable t/uni006F a default event that is p/uni006Fssible within the next 12 m/uni006Fnths. Effective interest is calculated /uni006Fn the gr/uni006Fss carrying am/uni006Funt /uni006Ff the l/uni006Fan receivable.

## Stage 2: Lifetime ECL - n/uni006Ft credit impaired

Where a l/uni006Fan receivable credit risk has increased significantly since initial rec/uni006Fgniti/uni006Fn, but is n/uni006Ft credit impaired, the l/uni006Fss all/uni006Fwance is based /uni006Fn the l/uni006Fan receivable lifetime ECL. F/uni006Fr these l/uni006Fan receivables, the Gr/uni006Fup rec/uni006Fgnises as a c/uni006Fllective pr/uni006Fvisi/uni006Fn a lifetime ECL (i.e. reflecting the remaining term /uni006Ff the l/uni006Fans receivable). ## Stage 3: Lifetime ECL - credit impaired

Where there is /uni006Fbjective evidence that the l/uni006Fan receivable has bec/uni006Fme credit impaired, the l/uni006Fss all/uni006Fwance is based /uni006Fn the l/uni006Fan receivable lifetime ECL. Effective interest is calculated /uni006Fn the net carrying am/uni006Funt /uni006Ff the financial instrument.

F/uni006Fr financial assets measured at fair value thr/uni006Fugh /uni006Fther c/uni006Fmprehensive inc/uni006Fme, the l/uni006Fss all/uni006Fwance is rec/uni006Fgnised within /uni006Fther c/uni006Fmprehensive inc/uni006Fme. In all /uni006Fther cases, the l/uni006Fss all/uni006Fwance is rec/uni006Fgnised in pr/uni006Ffit /uni006Fr l/uni006Fss.

## 6.2 All/uni006Fwance f/uni006Fr expected credit l/uni006Fsses

The Gr/uni006Fup has hist/uni006Frically ad/uni006Fpted an /uni006Fff-balance sheet l/uni006Fan funding m/uni006Fdel which resulted in relatively l/uni006Fw l/uni006Fan receivables /uni006Fn balance sheet. - ∞ B/uni006F/uni006Fk 4 - Wisr Freed/uni006Fm Trust 2022-1 - 99% Stage 1

N/uni006Fte 6. L/uni006Fan receivables | 6.2 All/uni006Fwance f/uni006Fr expected credit l/uni006Fsses (c/uni006Fnt.)

- ∞ B/uni006F/uni006Fk 5 - Wisr Finance - 92% Stage 1. This b/uni006F/uni006Fk c/uni006Fnsists /uni006Ff seas/uni006Fned, m/uni006Fstly legacy l/uni006Fan receivables which didn't qualify f/uni006Fr sale t/uni006F funding partners.

Credit l/uni006Fss refers t/uni006F the instance whereby a c/uni006Funterparty defaults /uni006Fn its c/uni006Fntractual /uni006Fbligati/uni006Fns resulting in financial l/uni006Fss t/uni006F the gr/uni006Fup. Default is defined as l/uni006Fan receivables which are at least 90 days past due. A significant increase in credit risk is defined as l/uni006Fan receivables which are at least 30 days past due.

The Gr/uni006Fup calculates ECL using three main c/uni006Fmp/uni006Fnents, the exp/uni006Fsure at default (EAD), the pr/uni006Fbability /uni006Ff default (PD) and the l/uni006Fss given default (LGD).

The EAD represents the t/uni006Ftal value the Gr/uni006Fup is exp/uni006Fsed t/uni006F when the l/uni006Fan receivable defaults. The 12-m/uni006Fnth ECL is calculated by multiplying the 12-m/uni006Fnth EAD, PD and LGD. Lifetime ECL is calculated using the lifetime PD instead.

The 12-m/uni006Fnth and lifetime PDs represent the pr/uni006Fbability /uni006Ff default /uni006Fccurring /uni006Fver the next 12 m/uni006Fnths and the remaining maturity /uni006Ff the l/uni006Fan receivable respectively. The LGD represents the unrec/uni006Fvered p/uni006Frti/uni006Fn /uni006Ff the EAD taking int/uni006F acc/uni006Funt any applicable rec/uni006Fvery /uni006Ff the l/uni006Fan receivable.

The Gr/uni006Fup /uni006Friginates l/uni006Fan receivables /uni006Ff 3, 5, and 7 year maturities t/uni006F Australian c/uni006Fnsumers. These l/uni006Fans are retained t/uni006F maturity within the Wisr Wareh/uni006Fuse Trust N/uni006F. 1, Wisr Wareh/uni006Fuse Trust N/uni006F. 2, Wisr Freed/uni006Fm Trust 2021-1 and Wisr Freed/uni006Fm Trust 2022-1.

The all/uni006Fwance f/uni006Fr ECL assessment requires a degree /uni006Ff estimati/uni006Fn and judgement. ## 12.1 Right-/uni006Ff-use assets

A right-/uni006Ff-use asset is rec/uni006Fgnised at the c/uni006Fmmencement date /uni006Ff a lease. The right-/uni006Ff-use asset is measured at c/uni006Fst, which c/uni006Fmprises the initial am/uni006Funt /uni006Ff the lease liability, adjusted f/uni006Fr, as

applicable, any lease payments made at /uni006Fr bef/uni006Fre the c/uni006Fmmencement date net /uni006Ff any lease incentives received, any initial direct c/uni006Fsts incurred, and, except where included in the c/uni006Fst /uni006Ff invent/uni006Fries, an estimate /uni006Ff c/uni006Fsts expected t/uni006F be incurred f/uni006Fr dismantling and rem/uni006Fving the underlying asset, and rest/uni006Fring the site /uni006Fr asset.

## 12.2 Lease liabilities

A lease liability is rec/uni006Fgnised at the c/uni006Fmmencement date /uni006Ff a lease. Lease liabilities are measured at am/uni006Frtised c/uni006Fst using the effective interest meth/uni006Fd. an extensi/uni006Fn /uni006Fpti/uni006Fn, /uni006Fr n/uni006Ft exercise a terminati/uni006Fn /uni006Fpti/uni006Fn, if there is a significant event /uni006Fr significant change in circumstances.

The Gr/uni006Fup enters int/uni006F derivative financial instruments (interest rate swaps) t/uni006F manage its exp/uni006Fsure t/uni006F interest rate risk.

## 32.9 Interest rate risk

Interest rate risk is the risk that the Gr/uni006Fup will experience deteri/uni006Frati/uni006Fn in its financial p/uni006Fsiti/uni006Fn as interest rates change /uni006Fver time. ## Interest rate swap c/uni006Fntracts

Under interest rate swap c/uni006Fntracts, the Gr/uni006Fup agrees t/uni006F exchange the difference between fixed and fl/uni006Fating rate interest am/uni006Funts calculated /uni006Fn agreed n/uni006Fti/uni006Fnal principal am/uni006Funts. Such c/uni006Fntracts enable the Gr/uni006Fup t/uni006F mitigate the cash fl/uni006Fw exp/uni006Fsures /uni006Fn its variable rate b/uni006Frr/uni006Fwings.

The Gr/uni006Fup designates the interest rate swap c/uni006Fntracts as cash fl/uni006Fw hedges. As the critical terms /uni006Ff the interest rate swap c/uni006Fntracts and their c/uni006Frresp/uni006Fnding hedged items are the same, the Gr/uni006Fup perf/uni006Frms a qualitative assessment /uni006Ff effectiveness and it is expected that the value /uni006Ff the interest rate swap c/uni006Fntracts and the value /uni006Ff the c/uni006Frresp/uni006Fnding hedged items will systematically change in /uni006Fpp/uni006Fsite directi/uni006Fn in resp/uni006Fnse t/uni006F m/uni006Fvements in the underlying interest rates. The main s/uni006Furce /uni006Ff hedge ineffectiveness in these hedge relati/uni006Fnships is the effect /uni006Ff the c/uni006Funterparty and the Gr/uni006Fup's /uni006Fwn credit risk /uni006Fn the fair value /uni006Ff the interest rate swap c/uni006Fntracts, which is n/uni006Ft reflected in the fair value /uni006Ff the hedged item attributable t/uni006F the change in interest rates. /uni004Fther s/uni006Furces /uni006Ff ineffectiveness include the re-designati/uni006Fn /uni006Ff amended interest rate swap c/uni006Fntracts, which have a n/uni006Fn-zer/uni006F fair value at incepti/uni006Fn /uni006Ff the hedge relati/uni006Fnship.

N/uni006Fte 32. Financial risk management (c/uni006Fnt.)

## Report on the Audit of the Financial Report

## Opinion

We have audited the financial report of Wisr Limited (the 'Company and its subsidiaries (collectively known as the 'Group' ), which comprises the consolidated statement of financial position as at 30 June 2022, the consolidated statement of profit or loss and other comprehensive income, the consolidated statement of changes in equity and the consolidated statement of cash flows for the year then ended, and notes to the financial report, including a summary of significant accounting policies and the directors' declaration.

In our opinion the accompanying financial report of the Group, is in accordance with the Corporations Act 2001, including:

- Giving a true and fair view of the Group's financial position as at 30 June 2022 and of its financial performance for the year ended on that date; and
- (ii) Complying with Australian Accounting Standards and the Corporations Regulations 2001 .

## Basis for opinion

We conducted our audit in accordance with Australian Auditing Standards \_ Our responsibilities under those standards are further described in the Auditor's responsibilities for the audit of the Financial Report section of our report. We are independent of the Group in accordance with the Corporations Act 2001 and the ethical requirements of the Accounting Professional and Ethical Standards Board's APES 110 Code of Ethics for Professional Accountants (including Independence Standards) (the Code) that are relevant to our audit of the financial report in Australia.  We have also fulfilled our other ethical responsibilities in accordance with the Code

We confirm that the independence declaration required by the Corporations Act 2001, which has been given to the directors of the Company, would be in the same terms if given to the directors as at the time of this auditor' s report.

We believe that the audit evidence we have obtained is sufficient and appropriate to provide a basis for our opinion.
